# Agents · R · part 16

932 entries, resolve-challenger to review. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **resolve-challenger**: Adversarial challenger for /resolve fix designs. Challenges each fix with configuration counterexamples, blast radius gaps, and regression vectors. Never fixes code. ([magpiexyz-lab/TipGuard](https://github.com/magpiexyz-lab/TipGuard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/resolve-challenger.md))

Arbeitet je Block gegen einen benannten Messstand, belegt jede Aussage mit Datei und Zeile oder mit einer ausgeführten Maske, und veröffentlicht erst nach dem Ja des Tores. Greppt zusätzlich nach Ausschließlichkeitsaussagen. Liefert eine Tabelle, kein Urteil; der Block geht erst ins Tor, wenn sie leer ist. Writes retro.md and spec.md for a self-improvement job. ([maxwellmattryan/forge](https://github.com/maxwellmattryan/forge/blob/HEAD/agents/retro.md) · ★1)
- **retro**: After a feature is finished or abandoned, records what went wrong as incidents, dead ends and proposed lessons, without editing any rules. Use at the end of /next-feature, or when a cap was hit. ([RayanAlyasi/AntiSpam-Bot](https://github.com/RayanAlyasi/AntiSpam-Bot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/retro.md) · ★1)
- **retro**: The hill-climbing loop. Reads the retro evidence digest (QA failures, agent log, failing evals, past fixes), finds repeated failure modes, and writes a proposal of at most 3 concrete edits to the harness or the agent. Proposes only; never edits the files it proposes to change. ([rishikeshF/self-improving-agent-harness](https://github.com/rishikeshF/self-improving-agent-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/retro.md) · ★1)
- **retro**: Runs the blameless retro over one finished run directory - what each gate caught, what leaked past the gate that should have caught it, grouped by class, with one proposed change per class as a diff, an executable guard before any prose rule. Launched without CLAUDE.md, so the dispatch must carry everything it needs. Proposes only; never edits rules, skills or profiles. Use for the retro node of any playbook. ([ShonP/graph-engineering](https://github.com/ShonP/graph-engineering/blob/HEAD/agents/retro.md) · ★1)
- **retro**: 現行の正準入口は `session-wrapup start|stop`。保存と起票は `issue-inbox`、振り返りは `session-retrospective`、期限付き試行は `feedback-trial` が担当する。 以下の旧 `wrapup-stop-gate` / `wrapup-session-start` による説明は、引き続き使える互換入口と移行の経緯を示す。 実装の配置と引数は[責務一覧](agent-libraries.md)を参照する。 ([tarotene/dotfiles](https://github.com/tarotene/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/retro.md) · ★1)
- **retro**: Retrospective agent - analyzes mission data and produces structured retro report ([theaiteam-dev/the-ai-team-plugin](https://github.com/theaiteam-dev/the-ai-team-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/retro.md) · ★1)
- **retro**: PR のレビュー指摘と CI の失敗から、仕組みで防げたはずの誤りを docs/feedback/LESSONS.md に記録する。/retro から使う。 ([tkc310/software-factory](https://github.com/tkc310/software-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/skills/software-factory/templates/claude/agents/retro.md) · ★1)
- **retro**: Run a weekly engineering retrospective.
